- **Delegation**: Parallelize work via six patterns: built-in agents (Algorithm/Explore/Plan/general-purpose via Task), worktree-isolated agents (conflict-free parallel edits), background agents (run_in_background, non-blocking), custom agents (ComposeAgent → Task), agent teams (TeamCreate + TaskCreate + SendMessage for multi-turn coordination), parallel task dispatch. Two-tier: lightweight (haiku, max_turns=3, one-shot) vs full (multi-step). Decision rule: agents need to talk or share state → Teams; independent one-shot → Subagents. Auto-invoked by Algorithm at 3+ workstreams. USE WHEN parallel execution, age… ([fadrienne/Apps](https://github.com/fadrienne/Apps/tree/HEAD/Personal_AI_Infrastructure/LifeOS/install/skills/Delegation))

- **Delegation Discipline**: Delegate work to subagents, background tasks, or parallel workers effectively - and verify their outputs before relying on them. Activate when spawning agents, fanning out searches or reviews, orchestrating multi-agent workflows, running background tasks, or incorporating any output produced outside your own context. Trigger signals: "use agents for this", "fan out", "in parallel", a task too large for one context, or noticing you're about to restate a subagent's claim as your own finding. ([ralfyishere/rules-with-receipts](https://github.com/ralfyishere/rules-with-receipts/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/delegation-discipline) · ★2)
- **delegation-and-empowerment**: Develops the daily practice of effective delegation, accountability diagnosis, and team empowerment. Covers assessing which tasks require personal attention, clarifying scope and context for delegated work, allowing productive struggle, recognising and correcting micromanagement, and leading through influence rather than control. Use when feeling overwhelmed by operational tasks, noticing accountability gaps on a team, receiving feedback about micromanagement, transitioning from individual contributor to manager, or leading a team with more expertise than oneself. ([fatihguner/foreman](https://github.com/fatihguner/foreman/tree/HEAD/plugins/foreman/skills/delegation-and-empowerment) · ★52)
- **delegation-and-review**: Rules for delegating work to subagents and judging what comes back — when to spawn vs. do it yourself, how to write a dispatch packet, how to review agent-produced changes without rubber-stamping, when to retry vs. change approach vs. ask the user, and how to hand off long-running work across sessions. Load when about to spawn a subagent or write a dispatch prompt, when fanning out parallel work, when the diff you are reviewing was produced by an agent, or when work will outlive this session. Do NOT load for small single-context tasks — just do those under operational-rigor. ([F-e-u-e-r/opus-pack](https://github.com/F-e-u-e-r/opus-pack/tree/HEAD/ops-pack/skills/delegation-and-review) · ★2)

- **deliberate-practice**: Deliberate Practice — Anders Ericsson's theory of deliberate practice. - **delivery-review**: 基于 git-notes 的双 Agent 交付协作工作流（loop 模式），按风险分级路由。 当用户说"交付"、"审查"、"review"、"验收"、"完成任务"、"检查改动"、"code-review"时激活。 流程：上下文确认 → 方案（双轨+用户选择，大改动含产品视角审查）→ 修复 Agent(delivery-fixer) ↔ 审查 Agent(delivery-reviewer，隔离只读)循环迭代 → 人工验收（裁决权归用户）→ 交付报告。 分级路由：trivial 快车道（一次确认+主会话直改+轻量审查，hook 机械校验兜底）；standard/critical 完整流程。 溯源链：对话实录（git-notes）→ 审查轨迹（review.log）→ 材料快照（work/material-rN.md）→ git diff 基线，四层可回查。 核心元认知：你不知道远比你知道的要重要的多——AI 必须声明哪些是确定的、哪些不确定、哪些根本没检查到。 注意：执行请走 /delivery-review 命令，它用状态机强制闭环；本 skill 提供方法论与数据模型参考。 ([xiaoxiao-svg/delivery-review-plugin](https://github.com/xiaoxiao-svg/delivery-review-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/delivery-review))
